5/13/2025,  Site Note:
Went to the lady's hosue in Crete, the unit was removed from cabinet, sitting on a counter. Plugged in the unit, the cavity light and display came on, the turntable continued turning with the door open, whic is not supposed to be. Brought unit to shop.

5/12/2025, Work Done:
Replaced control board, the symptom did not go away. Traced wiring diagram, all seemed OK. Contacted Tech Support, traced cirrcuit wiring diagram with Tech Support to make sure there were no problem with interlocks. In the process, marked the interlock switches. Apparenty the interlock switches were OK. The Tech Supported suggested that the new control board could be bad, and get a different one. The project is waiting for parts. K

7/14/2025, Repair Progress:
Replaced the control board for the second time, the problem did not go away. When tried to operate the unit, it tripped the breaker. Trace wires, find the problem. Update customer, work on the unit this week, update customer next Tuesday. K

Whirlpool called about this customer and asked to call the customer. Customer was updated. K

7/22/2025, Repair Report.
Replaced the control board for the third time, the unit still did the same as before and the turntable did not stop turning. Traced the wires to each intrelock and to the turn table motor, all checked out. The schematic which came with the unit did not match the wiring harness exactly. Some wire colors were switched. The unit tripped the shop breaker during testing. It was revealed that the AC line going to the high voltage transformer had cracked insulation and shorted to the frame inside a hole between the transformer and control board. The shorted area was not easily visible.  Will report this to Whirlpool. K-GP
Also, called the customer and updated her. K

7/25/2025, Final Repair and Assessment:
Over the range Maytag microwave with problem of turn table turning while door is open. Also, the unit stopped working and tripped the electrical circuit breaker. Upon extended close examination and trouble shooting, it was discovered that insulation of the high voltage wire to the transformer has disintegrated. This problem was hidden and not easily visible until each wire was traced for the faults causing the problem. It is possible that more insulation deterioration might be present in the wiring harness which has made this troubleshooting difficult. At this point, this unit is not safe to be repaired and returned to customer and Metro TV cannot warranty the repair of this unit with the existing wiring harness.

Pictures of the disintegrated wire and short circuiting to the chassis are attached.

09/24 WPL is refunding the customer and says for us to discontinue repair efforts. Sending affidavit dk

6/13/2025, Repair Summary. 
Unit came with the complaint of randon shutting off and arcing inside the cavity in microwave function. Replaced the contro board and the unit was thoroughly examined and put to extensive testing in the shop. The function was restored and did not shut off randomly. The arcing sound could be due to food matter splashing on the broiler elements, sounding as arcing. No arcing was observed. There are food burn particles baked on the broiler heating elements and reflector panel which cannot be removed without using abrassive matrial which may damage the reflector panel shiny surface. Therefore the baked food particles were not scrubbed. The customer is advised to cover the food inside the microwave before cooking in the microwave function. If the randon shutting off happens again, it might be due to insufficient air curculation as the unit gets hot with all the functions it performs.

Hello:

Thank you for the follow-up.


Here is a summary of our notes and observations:

The customer complain was that the TV randomly loses audio and video during operation, screen turns black, unit turns off and does not turn on after that.

When we checked the TV, it was not turning on initially, standby light was not blinking. After opening the unit, we tried resetting the cables to see if any effect. The standby light somehow came on and blinked 4 times, but no video or audio. We proceeded with replacing parts one at a time, starting with power supply. When power supply was replaced, the TV turned on, video and audio came on and the unit functioned. Then we replaced the main board and observed no effect. Then  TV was reassembled, put to test, it worked OK. When the assembled unit was placed on its wall bracket, the TV did not turn on and the standby light blinked for times, as if nothing was done to the TV. The customer said that this is what the TV was doing initially, going blank, and not turning on. So, we disassembled the TV, removed the new parts and reinstalled the original parts, then called SquareTrade to update on the repair.

 

Below are answers to your question.


-Yes, we took notes of the issue, as explained above.

-Yes, we took pictures of the unit model and serial numbers, attached.

-Both parts were replaced, one at a time, and the results are explained above.

-The TV has additional hardware components such as additional seemingly dedicated power supply board for LED drive, LED driver board, and T-Con. It is hard to predict which component is failing. Also, the panel could be a suspect too. So, with this model of Sony TV, it is hard to pinpoint the fault source. This is a question for the Sony Technical Support Team.

-No parts were missing; two parts were sent which were received.


I hope the above note helps.

7/17/2025, TL;DR 5A motor
Shop Note:
The unit has a capacitor start two speed with internal thermally protected motor at 5 Amp rated current draw. It starts and runs by itself when turned on, but draws over 8 Amp current in high speed setting which indicates excessive internal friction due to bearing surfaces in the armature. It needs front and rear bearings/bushings replaced along with the 10 uF cap. The fan was operated in the shop, it turned itself off when the external temperature exceeded 90 degree C. The fan motor needs bearings replaced. If motor parts are not available, the whole motor needs replaced along with the capacitor.  K-GP

9/22/2025, Repair Progress:
The unit will not run more than a minute or not complete one minute of operation. The lower mag was getting excessively hot, openning the mag TCO. The lower mag waveguide was contaminated with grease and baked debris. The upper mag high voltage transformer was shorted. Replaced lower mag and the upper mag tranformer. Upper mag was not heating. Moved the lower mag to the upper mag position. Cleaned cavity and filter. Secured connections, checked operation OK. Unit Done. K-N.

9/24/2025, Final Repair Summary:
Unit came to shop dead; line fuse open, magnetrons over drawing current, debris build up under tray, interlock not fuctioning properly. Replaced defective parts, clenaed cavity, cleaned air filter, checked operation OK. Unit Done.

9/24/25, Note:
This unit had a marginal high voltage transformer in the top mag circuit. The transfomer shorted out during testing. I was able to find a used transformer to install in this unit. Unless we give them a new estimate, to keep their cost down, I traded the transformer with one of the existing possibly salvageable mags. Should we give them a new higher estimate and install new mag, or leave it as it? The unit is working OK now.

";"DG94-05212A";;;;;;;;"DG94-04104A";"CRM";;;;;;"#Assy Ped 18779";;;;;;;;"Drawer Assy 17225";"10/20/25";;;;;
"Vizio";"tv";"XVT553SV";;;"PICTURE DOES NOT COME ON, INDICATOR NEAR LOGO ON BOTTOM BLINKS, PRIOR THE HDMI DID NOT SEEM TO WORK.";"no parts available, okay to dispose dk";"09/11 okay to dispose dk

9/8/2025, Shop Note:
Vizio TV came with not going past displaying the Vizio logo and rebooting. The standby light in this TV is the Vizio label on the middle bottom of the TV frame. When plugged in, the standby light flashes with white and amber colors alternately and continues nearly a minute, then the Vizio logo comes on briefly and goes off, the standby light flashes white and amber again. 

Isolated the LED driver board, it did not change anything. The problem points to main board, power supply, or both. If replacing these two boards did not fix it. Then it could be the panel.
Get main board and power supply, or repair kit.
Check the part availability and give estimate.

10/07/25 Replaced parts, still doing same. Called TAG. They suggested to get heater and drain pump. Heater out, drain pump shorted. Case #18470715 geo

A pump is a motor A motor is coiled wire. Coiled wire will be shorted. A shorted motor is NOT a valid reason for changing or descriping motor defect. UNLESS seperate coils are shorted.
The term ""shorted"" should not be used and an impedance value should be recorded. 10/15/25TB

10/21/2025, Final Repair:
TV came in power cycling, backlight flash then went dark. This TV has main board and power supply boards connected as combo unit and three segment address bar. Disassembled, isolated panel from main/PS combo, it did not change the condition, still power cycling. Isolated FFC from main, still no effect. Separated main from power supply, the backlight stayed on. This condition pointed to bad main. Replaced the main, it turned that the new main was defective. Got second main. TV came one for part of reboot process, then the screen went blank. Isolated components, the IR sensor was also defective. With new IR sensor and new main/PS combo, the TV operated normally. Reset the option bytes, updated software to 2000.2. Connected to internet, OP OK Unit Done.
